I now have a complete picture. Here's the honest state of the game:

**What's already there (and it's a lot):** a full survival loop — 28 blocks, 46 items, a 7-tier tool/weapon/armor progression, farming, animal breeding, cooking, beds/respawn, a 240s day/night cycle with dynamic sky + ambient occlusion, **caves**, ores, ~120 generated houses, trees, 5 (cosmetic) biomes, static water, 6 mobs with AI, generative spatial audio, and first/third-person with held-item models.

**The conspicuous gaps** — the things that would move the needle most:
- **No "juice"** — zero particles (no break/death/splash/dust), no weather, no stars/moon/clouds.
- **No block light** — caves are lit like the surface; no torches, no darkness, no tension.
- **No storage** — you're capped at 36 inventory slots; no chests.
- **No exploration payoff** — caves and houses have no loot; **no endgame/goal** past mining diamond.
- **No fluid hazard** — water is harmless, no lava, no drowning.
- **No ranged combat** — no bows/projectiles.

My honest take: since we just did a visual polish pass, the highest-ROI, lowest-risk continuation is a **"juice & atmosphere"** pass (pure procedural rendering/audio — no save-format or worldgen risk, and it makes every action feel alive). But if you'd rather add *gameplay depth*, chests+loot or cave-lighting are the strongest picks. This is genuinely your roadmap call, so let me get your direction before I design anything.

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { buildGeometryRegion, createBlockAtlasTexture, voxelRaycast, VoxelWorld } from "@/lib/world";

13	const scratchEye = new THREE.Vector3();

17	export type CreateRendererResult = { ok: true; renderer: GameRenderer } | { ok: false; error: string };
18	
19	/**
20	 * Maps simulation state to Three.js every frame in sync(): camera from
21	 * yaw/pitch, world mesh from the voxel data (region-windowed, rebuilt when the
22	 * player crosses a RENDER_GRID boundary or state.worldMeshDirty is set), mob
23	 * models keyed by mob id, the held item, the crack overlay, and day-night
24	 * lighting. Owns every GPU resource and frees them in dispose().
25	 */
26	export class GameRenderer {
27	  private readonly mount: HTMLElement;
28	  private readonly scene: THREE.Scene;
29	  private readonly camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
30	  private readonly webgl: THREE.WebGLRenderer;
31	  private readonly sun: THREE.DirectionalLight;
32	  private readonly hemiLight: THREE.HemisphereLight;
33	  private readonly daySky = new THREE.Color(0x8bc2ff);
34	  private readonly nightSky = new THREE.Color(0x06111f);
35	  private readonly liveSky = new THREE.Color(0x8bc2ff);
36	  private readonly worldMaterial: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
37	  private worldMesh: THREE.Mesh;
38	  private currentRegionX = Number.NaN;
39	  private currentRegionZ = Number.NaN;
40	  private readonly heldItem: HeldItemView;
41	  private readonly crackOverlay: CrackOverlayView;
42	  private readonly mobVisuals: MobVisuals;
43	  private readonly playerVisuals: PlayerVisuals;
44	
45	  /** WebGL context creation can fail (blocked, unsupported) — surface it instead of throwing. */
46	  static create(mount: HTMLElement): CreateRendererResult {
47	    try {
48	      return { ok: true, renderer: new GameRenderer(mount) };
49	    } catch (error) {
50	      return { ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to initialize WebGL renderer" };
51	    }
52	  }
53	
54	  private constructor(mount: HTMLElement) {
55	    this.mount = mount;
56	    this.scene = new THREE.Scene();
57	    this.scene.background = this.liveSky;
58	    this.scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(this.liveSky, 30, 200);
59	
60	    this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight, 0.1, 2000);
61	    this.webgl = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
62	    this.webgl.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
63	    this.webgl.setSize(mount.clientWidth, mount.clientHeight);
64	    mount.appendChild(this.webgl.domElement);
65	
66	    this.hemiLight = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xd7efff, 0x9a907f, 1.18);
67	    this.scene.add(this.hemiLight);
68	
69	    this.sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff4da, 1.28);
70	    this.sun.position.set(40, 95, 24);
71	    this.scene.add(this.sun);
72	
73	    this.worldMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
74	      map: createBlockAtlasTexture(),
75	      vertexColors: true,
76	      roughness: 0.88,
77	      metalness: 0.02,
78	      side: THREE.DoubleSide
79	    });
80	    this.worldMesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BufferGeometry(), this.worldMaterial);
81	    this.scene.add(this.worldMesh);
82	    this.scene.add(this.camera);
83	
84	    this.heldItem = createHeldItemView(this.camera);
85	    this.crackOverlay = createCrackOverlay(this.scene);
86	    this.mobVisuals = createMobVisuals(this.scene);
87	    this.playerVisuals = createPlayerVisuals(this.scene);
88	  }
89	
90	  get domElement(): HTMLCanvasElement {
91	    return this.webgl.domElement;
92	  }
93	
94	  /** Triangles drawn in the last render() — lets E2E tests prove the scene renders without reading pixels. */
95	  renderedTriangles(): number {
96	    return this.webgl.info.render.triangles;
97	  }
98	
99	  /** Pulls everything visible from the simulation state. Call once per frame, then render(). */
100	  sync(state: GameState, timeMs: number): void {
101	    this.syncCamera(state);
102	    this.syncWorldMesh(state);
103	    this.heldItem.update(state.inventory[state.selectedSlot], {
104	      timeMs,
105	      miningActive: state.mining.targetKey !== "",
106	      moveFactor: state.player.onGround ? Math.min(1, Math.hypot(state.player.velocity.x, state.player.velocity.z) / WALK_SPEED) : 0,
107	      visible: state.cameraMode === "first"
108	    });
109	    this.crackOverlay.update(state.mining, state.world);
110	    this.mobVisuals.sync(state.mobs, timeMs);
111	    this.playerVisuals.sync(state, timeMs);
112	    this.syncDayNight(state);
113	  }
114	
115	  render(): void {
116	    this.webgl.render(this.scene, this.camera);
117	  }
118	
119	  /** One-shot swing for attack clicks (hit or miss) — held item and body arm. */
120	  triggerSwing(): void {
121	    this.heldItem.triggerSwing();
122	    this.playerVisuals.triggerSwing();
123	  }
124	
125	  /** Applies a skin preset's palette to the player body (live recolor). */
126	  setPlayerSkin(palette: PlayerPalette): void {
127	    this.playerVisuals.setPalette(palette);
128	  }
129	
130	  handleResize(): void {
131	    this.camera.aspect = this.mount.clientWidth / this.mount.clientHeight;
132	    this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
133	    this.webgl.setSize(this.mount.clientWidth, this.mount.clientHeight);
134	  }
135	
136	  dispose(): void {
137	    this.playerVisuals.dispose();
138	    this.mobVisuals.dispose();
139	    this.crackOverlay.dispose();
140	    this.heldItem.dispose();
141	    this.scene.remove(this.worldMesh);
142	    this.worldMesh.geometry.dispose();
143	    this.worldMaterial.dispose();
144	    this.webgl.dispose();
145	    this.mount.removeChild(this.webgl.domElement);
146	  }
147	
148	  private syncCamera(state: GameState): void {
149	    const { position, yaw, pitch } = state.player;
150	    scratchEye.set(position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
151	
152	    let distance = 0;
153	    if (state.cameraMode !== "first") {
154	      // Clamp the boom against terrain so walls never occlude the player.
155	      const dir = cameraOffsetDirection(state.cameraMode, yaw, pitch, scratchDir);
156	      const hit = voxelRaycast(state.world, scratchEye, dir, THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE);
157	      distance = hit ? Math.max(0, hit.distance - THIRD_PERSON_MARGIN) : THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE;
158	    }
159	
160	    const pose = computeCameraPose(state.cameraMode, scratchEye.x, scratchEye.y, scratchEye.z, yaw, pitch, distance, scratchPose);
161	    this.camera.position.set(pose.posX, pose.posY, pose.posZ);
162	    this.camera.rotation.order = "YXZ";
163	    this.camera.rotation.y = pose.yaw;
164	    this.camera.rotation.x = pose.pitch;
165	  }
166	
167	  private syncWorldMesh(state: GameState): void {
168	    const regionX = Math.floor(state.player.position.x / RENDER_GRID) * RENDER_GRID;
169	    const regionZ = Math.floor(state.player.position.z / RENDER_GRID) * RENDER_GRID;
170	    if (!state.worldMeshDirty && regionX === this.currentRegionX && regionZ === this.currentRegionZ) return;
171	
172	    state.worldMeshDirty = false;
173	    this.currentRegionX = regionX;
174	    this.currentRegionZ = regionZ;
175	
176	    this.rebuildWorldMesh(state.world, regionX, regionZ);
177	  }
178	
179	  private rebuildWorldMesh(world: VoxelWorld, regionX: number, regionZ: number): void {
180	    const geometry = buildGeometryRegion(world, regionX - RENDER_RADIUS, regionX + RENDER_RADIUS, regionZ - RENDER_RADIUS, regionZ + RENDER_RADIUS);
181	    this.scene.remove(this.worldMesh);
182	    this.worldMesh.geometry.dispose();
183	    this.worldMesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, this.worldMaterial);
184	    this.scene.add(this.worldMesh);
185	  }
186	
187	  private syncDayNight(state: GameState): void {
188	    const sunAngle = sunAngleAt(state.dayClock);
189	    const daylight = state.daylight;
190	
191	    this.sun.position.set(Math.cos(sunAngle) * 110, Math.sin(sunAngle) * 108, Math.sin(sunAngle * 0.7) * 80);
192	    this.sun.intensity = 0.2 + daylight * 1.2;
193	    this.hemiLight.intensity = 0.24 + daylight * 1.05;
194	
195	    this.liveSky.copy(this.nightSky).lerp(this.daySky, daylight);
196	    this.scene.fog?.color.copy(this.liveSky);
197	  }
198	}
199

200	    const clock = () => {
201	      const now = performance.now();
202	      pendingSeconds = Math.min(pendingSeconds + (now - last) / 1000, MAX_STEP_SECONDS * MAX_SUBSTEPS);
203	      last = now;
204	
205	      let frameSeconds = 0;
206	      while (pendingSeconds > 0) {
207	        const dt = Math.min(pendingSeconds, MAX_STEP_SECONDS);
208	        gameEngine.step(dt, input.input);
209	        pendingSeconds -= dt;
210	        frameSeconds += dt;
211	      }
212	
213	      for (const event of gameEngine.consumeEvents()) {
214	        if (event.type === "died") {
215	          input.clearKeys();
216	          if (document.pointerLockElement === renderer.domElement) document.exitPointerLock();
217	        }
218	        if (event.type === "respawned") input.clearKeys();
219	        if (event.type === "attackSwung") renderer.triggerSwing();
220	        if (event.type === "openedStation") {
221	          // A furnace opened the inventory from a mouse click — release the keys
222	          // and pointer lock the same way KeyI does on the DOM side.
223	          input.clearKeys();
224	          if (document.pointerLockElement === renderer.domElement) document.exitPointerLock();
225	        }
226	        if (event.type === "sleepDenied") {
227	          flashMessage(event.reason === "daylight" ? "You can only sleep at night" : "Monsters are nearby");
228	        }
229	        if (event.type === "pickedUp") {
230	          flashMessage(event.items.map((drop) => `+${drop.count} ${ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[drop.itemId]?.label ?? drop.itemId}`).join(", "));
231	        }
232	        audio.handleEvent(event);
233	      }
234	
235	      if (!minimap && minimapNodeRef.current) minimap = createMinimapRenderer(minimapNodeRef.current);
236	      // The minimap must read worldMeshDirty before renderer.sync clears it.
237	      minimap?.sync(gameEngine.state, now);
238	      renderer.sync(gameEngine.state, now);
239	      audio.sync(gameEngine.state, frameSeconds);

34	export type AudioSettings = {
35	  /** 0..1 — everything. */
36	  master: number;
37	  /** 0..1 — the ambient pad only, under master. */
38	  music: number;
39	  muted: boolean;
40	};
41	
42	export const DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS: AudioSettings = { master: 0.8, music: 0.6, muted: false };

44	/** The live WebAudio half, created lazily on unlock(). Injectable for tests. */
45	export type AudioGraph = {
46	  backend: SynthBackend;
47	  music: MusicPlayer;
48	  setVolumes(master: number, music: number): void;
49	  resume(): void;
50	  dispose(): void;
51	};

53	export type AudioDirector = {
54	  /** Builds/resumes the AudioContext. Must be called from a user gesture. */
55	  unlock(): void;
56	  handleEvent(event: GameEvent): void;
57	  /** Per-frame, after the engine step — drives all continuous sound. */
58	  sync(state: GameState, dt: number): void;
59	  setSettings(settings: AudioSettings): void;
60	  dispose(): void;
61	};
62	
63	/** Mining ticks per block — one per crack-overlay-ish stage. */
64	const MINING_TICK_STAGES = 4;
65	/** Touchdown speed that plays the landing thud at full volume. */
66	const FULL_LANDING_IMPACT = 12;
67	/** Seconds a newly entered biome must persist before the music follows it. */
68	const BIOME_HYSTERESIS_SECONDS = 5;
69	/** Music runs at quarter volume behind the pause menu. */
70	const PAUSE_DUCK = 0.25;
71	
72	/** The production graph: zzfx + AudioContext, imported only inside a gesture. */
73	async function createDefaultGraph(): Promise<AudioGraph> {
74	  // zzfx instantiates its own module-scope AudioContext on import, so the
75	  // import itself must wait for the unlock gesture (autoplay policy, SSR).
76	  const { ZZFX } = await import("zzfx");
77	  const ctx = new AudioContext();
78	  const compressor = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
79	  compressor.connect(ctx.destination);
80	  const masterGain = ctx.createGain();
81	  masterGain.connect(compressor);
82	  const sfxGain = ctx.createGain();
83	  sfxGain.connect(masterGain);
84	  const musicGain = ctx.createGain();
85	  musicGain.connect(masterGain);
86	
87	  const backend = createSynthBackend(ctx, sfxGain, (...params) => ZZFX.buildSamples(...params), ZZFX.sampleRate);
88	  const music = createMusicPlayer(ctx, musicGain, createMusicBrain());
89	
90	  return {
91	    backend,
92	    music,
93	    setVolumes(master, musicVolume) {
94	      masterGain.gain.value = master;
95	      musicGain.gain.value = musicVolume;
96	    },
97	    resume() {
98	      if (ctx.state === "suspended") void ctx.resume();
99	    },
100	    dispose() {
101	      music.dispose();
102	      backend.dispose();
103	      void ctx.close();
104	    }
105	  };
106	}
107	
108	export type AudioDirectorDeps = {
109	  createGraph?: () => Promise<AudioGraph> | AudioGraph;
110	  rng?: () => number;
111	};
112	
113	export function createAudioDirector(deps: AudioDirectorDeps = {}): AudioDirector {
114	  const createGraph = deps.createGraph ?? createDefaultGraph;
115	  const footsteps = createFootstepScheduler();
116	  const mobAmbience = createMobAmbienceScheduler(deps.rng);
117	
118	  let graph: AudioGraph | null = null;
119	  let unlocking = false;
120	  let disposed = false;
121	  let settings = DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS;
122	
123	  // Continuous-sound trackers, all derived from state in sync().
124	  let lastX = 0;
125	  let lastZ = 0;
126	  let hasLastPosition = false;
127	  let miningKey = "";
128	  let miningStage = 0;
129	  let miningTickSound = HIT_TICK_SOUNDS.stone;
130	  let miningHardness = 2;
131	  let stableBiome = BiomeId.Plains;
132	  let pendingBiome = BiomeId.Plains;
133	  let pendingBiomeSeconds = 0;
134	  let ducked = false;
135	
136	  const applyVolumes = (): void => {
137	    graph?.setVolumes(settings.muted ? 0 : settings.master, (ducked ? PAUSE_DUCK : 1) * settings.music);
138	  };
139	
140	  const surfaceGroupUnder = (state: GameState, fx: number, fy: number, fz: number) => {
141	    let block = state.world.get(fx, fy - 1, fz) as BlockId;
142	    if (block === BlockId.Air) block = state.world.get(fx, fy - 2, fz) as BlockId;
143	    return materialGroupFor(block);
144	  };
145	
146	  return {
147	    unlock() {
148	      if (disposed || graph || unlocking) {
149	        graph?.resume();
150	        return;
151	      }
152	      unlocking = true;
153	      void Promise.resolve(createGraph())
154	        .then((created) => {
155	          if (disposed) {
156	            created.dispose();
157	            return;
158	          }
159	          graph = created;
160	          // The constructor gesture may have expired by this microtask —
161	          // resume explicitly so the context reliably leaves "suspended".
162	          created.resume();
163	          applyVolumes();
164	        })
165	        .catch(() => {
166	          // Audio stays optional; the next gesture retries from scratch.
167	        })
168	        .finally(() => {
169	          unlocking = false;
170	        });
171	    },
172	
173	    handleEvent(event) {
174	      const backend = graph?.backend;
175	      if (!backend) return;
176	      switch (event.type) {
177	        case "blockBroken":
178	          backend.play(BREAK_SOUNDS[materialGroupFor(event.blockId)]);
179	          break;
180	        case "blockPlaced":
181	          backend.play(PLACE_SOUNDS[materialGroupFor(event.blockId)]);
182	          break;
183	        case "playerHurt":
184	          backend.play(HURT_SOUND);
185	          break;
186	        case "ateFood":
187	          backend.play(EAT_SOUND);
188	          break;
189	        case "jumped":
190	          backend.play(JUMP_SOUND);
191	          break;
192	        case "landed":
193	          backend.play(LAND_SOUND, { gain: Math.min(1.2, Math.max(0.3, event.impact / FULL_LANDING_IMPACT)) });
194	          break;
195	        case "mobAttacked":
196	          backend.play(MOB_ATTACK_SOUNDS[event.kind]);
197	          break;
198	        case "mobHit":
199	          backend.play(MOB_HIT_SOUND);
200	          break;
201	        case "mobDied":
202	          backend.play(MOB_DEATH_SOUND);
203	          break;
204	        case "died":
205	          backend.play(DEATH_SOUND);
206	          break;
207	        case "respawned":
208	          backend.play(RESPAWN_SOUND);
209	          break;
210	        case "sleepStarted":
211	          backend.play(SLEEP_SOUND);
212	          break;
213	        case "wokeUp":
214	          backend.play(WAKE_SOUND);
215	          break;
216	        case "tilledSoil":
217	          backend.play(TILL_SOUND);
218	          break;
219	        case "plantedSeed":
220	          backend.play(PLANT_SOUND);
221	          break;
222	        case "smelted":
223	          backend.play(SMELT_SOUND);
224	          break;
225	        case "mobFed":
226	          backend.play(MOB_FED_SOUND);
227	          break;
228	        case "mobBred":
229	          backend.play(MOB_BRED_SOUND);
230	          break;
231	      }
232	    },
233	
234	    sync(state, dt) {
235	      if (!graph) return;
236	      const { player, world } = state;
237	      const fx = Math.floor(player.position.x);
238	      const fy = Math.floor(player.position.y);
239	      const fz = Math.floor(player.position.z);
240	
241	      // Footsteps from actual movement deltas (knockback and walking alike).
242	      if (!hasLastPosition) {
243	        lastX = player.position.x;
244	        lastZ = player.position.z;
245	        hasLastPosition = true;
246	      }
247	      const dx = player.position.x - lastX;
248	      const dz = player.position.z - lastZ;
249	      lastX = player.position.x;
250	      lastZ = player.position.z;
251	      if (!state.paused && !state.isDead && footsteps.tick(player.onGround, dx, dz)) {
252	        graph.backend.play(FOOTSTEP_SOUNDS[surfaceGroupUnder(state, fx, fy, fz)]);
253	      }
254	
255	      // Mining hit ticks: one per quarter of the block's hardness.
256	      if (state.mining.targetKey !== miningKey) {
257	        miningKey = state.mining.targetKey;
258	        miningStage = 0;
259	        if (miningKey) {
260	          const [bx, by, bz] = miningKey.split(",").map(Number);
261	          const block = world.get(bx, by, bz) as BlockId;
262	          miningTickSound = HIT_TICK_SOUNDS[materialGroupFor(block)];
263	          miningHardness = BREAK_HARDNESS[block] ?? 2;
264	        }
265	      }
266	      if (miningKey && state.mining.progress < miningHardness) {
267	        const stage = Math.floor((state.mining.progress / miningHardness) * MINING_TICK_STAGES);
268	        if (stage > miningStage) {
269	          miningStage = stage;
270	          graph.backend.play(miningTickSound);
271	        }
272	      }
273	
274	      // Mob ambience, frozen with the simulation while paused.
275	      if (!state.paused) {
276	        const calls = mobAmbience.tick(dt, state.mobs, player.position.x, player.position.z, player.yaw);
277	        for (const call of calls) {
278	          graph.backend.play(MOB_AMBIENT_SOUNDS[call.kind], { gain: call.gain, pan: call.pan });
279	        }
280	      }
281	
282	      // Music follows daylight immediately and the biome with hysteresis, so
283	      // zigzagging a chunk border doesn't whipsaw the mood.
284	      const biomeHere = world.getBiome(fx, fz);
285	      if (biomeHere === stableBiome) {
286	        pendingBiomeSeconds = 0;
287	      } else if (biomeHere === pendingBiome) {
288	        pendingBiomeSeconds += dt;
289	        if (pendingBiomeSeconds >= BIOME_HYSTERESIS_SECONDS) stableBiome = biomeHere;
290	      } else {
291	        pendingBiome = biomeHere;
292	        pendingBiomeSeconds = 0;
293	      }
294	      graph.music.sync(dt, moodFor(state.daylight, stableBiome));
295	
296	      if (ducked !== state.paused) {
297	        ducked = state.paused;
298	        applyVolumes();
299	      }
300	    },
301	
302	    setSettings(next) {
303	      settings = next;
304	      applyVolumes();
305	    },
306	
307	    dispose() {
308	      disposed = true;
309	      graph?.dispose();
310	      graph = null;
311	    }
312	  };
313	}
314

# Juice & Atmosphere Feature Pass — Implementation Plan

## Verification of the integration points (confirmed against real code)

- `GameRenderer.sync(state, timeMs)` ends with `syncDayNight(state)` at `GameRenderer.ts:112/187`; sub-views are factories returning `{update/sync, dispose}` (`createMobVisuals` etc.), all disposed in `dispose()` (`:136`). No `handleEvent` method exists yet.
- Drain loop at `useMinecraftGame.ts:213-233` already calls `renderer.triggerSwing()` on `attackSwung` and `audio.handleEvent(event)`. `renderer.sync(...)` is called at `:238` with `now` (absolute `performance.now()`), `audio.sync(state, frameSeconds)` at `:239` with the accumulated frame dt.
- `blockBroken`/`blockPlaced` emitted at `mining.ts:93/124`; broken coords `bx,by,bz` are in scope (`:66-68`), placed coords `tx,ty,tz` in scope (`:104-106`). `mobDied` emitted at `GameEngine.ts:372` inside `removeMobAt`, with `mob.position` in scope.
- Engine step order (`GameEngine.ts:185-190`): `tickMining → tickDayNight → tickRandomBlocks → tickHostileSpawnDirector → tickMobs → tickBreeding`. New `tickWeather` slots immediately after `tickDayNight`.
- `GameState` is built inline at `GameEngine.ts:88-119`; `serialize()` (`:311-330`) hand-lists fields (no `weather` → automatically excluded from save). `GameSnapshot` (`state.ts:153`) is also hand-listed.
- Save round-trip e2e (`smoke.e2e.ts:160-179`) asserts `version === 3` and seed; adding a transient `state.weather` cannot affect it.
- `BLOCK_COLORS: Record<number,[r,g,b]>` (float RGB) at `blocks.ts:71`. Per-mob body color = `MOB_TEMPLATES[kind].modelArgs[0]` (a hex int, e.g. zombie `0x669e57`), `mobs.ts:13-66`. `MobKind = "sheep"|"chicken"|"horse"|"zombie"|"skeleton"|"spider"`.
- `world.getBiome(x,z): BiomeId` (`voxelWorld.ts:56`), `BiomeId` enum at `blocks.ts:36` (Plains/Desert/Ocean/Forest/Mountains). `world.seed: number` (`voxelWorld.ts:15`).
- Audio graph (`audioDirector.ts:73-106`) builds `ctx`, `masterGain`, `sfxGain`, `musicGain`; `backend` from `createSynthBackend(ctx, sfxGain, ...)`. `AudioGraph` is `{backend, music, setVolumes, resume, dispose}`. `handleEvent`/`sync` both early-return when `graph` is null. Tests inject a fake `createGraph`.
- Pure-module test convention: `bun:test`, deterministic, DOM-free (`spritePixels.test.ts`). Scripts: `lint`, `typecheck` (`next typegen && tsc --noEmit`), `format:check`, `test` (`bun test`), `build`, `test:e2e`.

Each slice below is independently green (lint/typecheck/test/build) and shippable.

## SLICE 1 — Particles

### 1a. Pure particle pool (DOM-free, unit-tested)

**New file: `lib/game/render/particlePool.ts`** — pure simulation, no Three.js import.

Data structure: Structure-of-Arrays over a fixed pool (cache-friendly, zero per-frame allocation, trivially mappable to GPU attribute buffers).

```ts
export const PARTICLE_CAP = 512;

export type ParticlePool = {
  readonly cap: number;
  /** Live count (slots [0..count) are active and front-packed each update). */
  count: number;
  // SoA buffers, length === cap
  px: Float32Array; py: Float32Array; pz: Float32Array;
  vx: Float32Array; vy: Float32Array; vz: Float32Array;
  age: Float32Array;   // seconds lived
  life: Float32Array;  // seconds total
  size: Float32Array;  // world units
  cr: Float32Array; cg: Float32Array; cb: Float32Array; // 0..1
  gravity: Float32Array; drag: Float32Array;            // per-particle
};

export type EmitParticle = {
  x: number; y: number; z: number;
  vx: number; vy: number; vz: number;
  life: number; size: number;
  r: number; g: number; b: number;
  gravity?: number; drag?: number;
};

export function createParticlePool(cap = PARTICLE_CAP): ParticlePool;

/** Spawns one particle. At cap, recycles slot 0 (oldest after compaction). */
export function spawnParticle(pool: ParticlePool, p: EmitParticle): void;

/** Integrates dt seconds, ages particles, retires expired by swap-removal
 *  (compacts live particles into [0..count)). dt clamped to a sane max. */
export function updateParticlePool(pool: ParticlePool, dt: number): void;
```

Integrator (representative):

```ts
export function updateParticlePool(pool: ParticlePool, dt: number): void {
  const d = Math.min(dt, 0.1); // guard background-tab jumps
  let i = 0;
  while (i < pool.count) {
    pool.age[i] += d;
    if (pool.age[i] >= pool.life[i]) {
      const last = pool.count - 1;            // swap-remove (order not significant)
      if (i !== last) copySlot(pool, last, i);
      pool.count = last;
      continue;                                // re-process the swapped-in slot
    }
    const dragF = 1 - pool.drag[i] * d;
    pool.vy[i] -= pool.gravity[i] * d;
    pool.vx[i] *= dragF; pool.vy[i] *= dragF; pool.vz[i] *= dragF;
    pool.px[i] += pool.vx[i] * d;
    pool.py[i] += pool.vy[i] * d;
    pool.pz[i] += pool.vz[i] * d;
    i += 1;
  }
}
```

Recycle-at-cap: when `count === cap`, `spawnParticle` overwrites slot 0 and leaves `count` at cap (cheapest "drop oldest"; precise ordering is not gameplay-relevant for cosmetic dust). `copySlot(pool, from, to)` is a private helper copying all SoA fields.

**New file: `lib/game/render/particlePool.test.ts`** (`bun:test`):
- `createParticlePool` → all buffers length `cap`, `count===0`.
- spawn fills sequential slots, increments `count`.
- `updateParticlePool` ages and integrates (position moves by `v*dt`, `vy` decreases by gravity).
- after `life` elapses, the particle is retired and `count` decreases.
- spawning past `cap` keeps `count === cap` (recycles, no overflow / no throw).
- determinism: same inputs → same buffers (no `Math.random` inside the pool; jitter is supplied by the caller).

### 1b. THREE factory

**New file: `lib/game/render/particleSystem.ts`** — wraps the pool in GPU resources.

**Representation choice: `THREE.Points` with a `BufferGeometry` (position + color attributes) and a `PointsMaterial { vertexColors, transparent, depthWrite:false, sizeAttenuation:true, map: roundSpriteTexture() }`.** Justification vs InstancedMesh for "a few hundred" particles:
- One draw call, one geometry; per-frame cost is copying SoA → two `Float32Array` attributes plus `needsUpdate`. No per-instance matrix math.
- A canvas-generated round/soft-square alpha sprite (procedural, zero binary assets, same pattern as `chromeTiles.ts`) gives shards/dust a soft look. Voxel "shards" read fine as fat points.
- `PointsMaterial.size` is screen-ish; combined with `sizeAttenuation` it gives distance falloff. (InstancedMesh of quads would allow true world-space size + rotation, but it is overkill and costs more CPU for the matrix writes; revisit only if shards must spin.)
- `points.frustumCulled = false` (camera-local emission near player), `points.raycast = () => {}` so particles never interfere with block/mob raycasts.

Factory signature (sibling of `createMobVisuals`):

```ts
export type ParticleSystem = {
  emitBurst(opts: BurstOptions): void;
  update(dtMs: number): void;   // converts to seconds, steps pool, uploads attrs
  dispose(): void;
};
export type BurstOptions = {
  x: number; y: number; z: number;
  count: number;
  color: [number, number, number];      // base 0..1 RGB
  speed: number;                          // initial speed magnitude
  spread?: number;                        // velocity randomization
  life?: [number, number];                // [min,max] seconds, randomized
  size?: number;
  gravity?: number; drag?: number;
  upBias?: number;                        // adds +y to velocity (dust rises a touch)
  colorJitter?: number;                   // ± per-channel variation
};

export function createParticleSystem(scene: THREE.Scene): ParticleSystem;
```

- Owns: `createParticlePool()`, a `BufferGeometry` with `position`/`color` attributes sized to `PARTICLE_CAP`, a `PointsMaterial` (with the procedural sprite texture), and the `THREE.Points`. Adds to `scene` in the factory, removes + disposes geometry/material/texture in `dispose()` (mirrors `mobVisuals.dispose`).
- `emitBurst` is the only place `Math.random()` is used (jitter on velocity/life/color), keeping the pool deterministic and unit-testable.
- `update(dtMs)` → `updateParticlePool(pool, dtMs/1000)`, then writes `pool.count` particles into the attribute arrays, sets `geometry.setDrawRange(0, pool.count)`, flags `needsUpdate`. No-ops cleanly when `count===0` (still safe under e2e).

### 1c. Event → burst mapping (renderer.handleEvent)

**Edit `GameRenderer.ts`:**
- Import `createParticleSystem`; add `private readonly particles: ParticleSystem` constructed in the ctor (`createParticleSystem(this.scene)`); dispose in `dispose()`.
- Track `private lastSyncMs = NaN` to derive `dtMs` inside `sync` (the prompt's recommended approach — derive dt in the renderer, since `sync` gets absolute `timeMs`):
  ```ts
  const dtMs = Number.isNaN(this.lastSyncMs) ? 16 : timeMs - this.lastSyncMs;
  this.lastSyncMs = timeMs;
  if (!state.paused) this.particles.update(dtMs);
  ```
  Place this in `sync` (skip the pool step while paused so particles freeze with the sim). Keep `dtMs` clamped inside `updateParticlePool` already.
- Add the NEW one-shot method (mirrors `triggerSwing`'s shape):

```ts
handleEvent(event: GameEvent): void {
  switch (event.type) {
    case "blockBroken": {
      const c = BLOCK_COLORS[event.blockId] ?? [0.6, 0.6, 0.6];
      this.particles.emitBurst({ x: event.x + 0.5, y: event.y + 0.5, z: event.z + 0.5,
        count: 10, color: c, speed: 3.2, spread: 1.0, gravity: 14, drag: 1.6,
        life: [0.35, 0.7], size: 0.16, colorJitter: 0.08 });
      break;
    }
    case "blockPlaced": {
      const c = BLOCK_COLORS[event.blockId] ?? [0.6, 0.6, 0.6];
      this.particles.emitBurst({ x: event.x + 0.5, y: event.y + 0.5, z: event.z + 0.5,
        count: 6, color: c, speed: 1.2, spread: 0.6, gravity: 6, drag: 2.2,
        life: [0.25, 0.45], size: 0.12, upBias: 0.6 });
      break;
    }
    case "mobDied": {
      const hex = MOB_TEMPLATES[event.kind].modelArgs[0] as number;
      this.particles.emitBurst({ x: event.x, y: event.y, z: event.z,
        count: 14, color: hexToRgb(hex), speed: 2.6, spread: 1.4,
        gravity: 10, drag: 1.4, life: [0.4, 0.8], size: 0.18 });
      break;
    }
    case "ateFood":
      this.particles.emitBurst({ ...this.playerEmitPoint(state?), count: 5,
        color: [0.82, 0.6, 0.4], speed: 1.6, spread: 0.8, gravity: 12,
        drag: 1.5, life: [0.25, 0.5], size: 0.1 });
      break;
    case "landed":
      this.particles.emitBurst({ ...groundUnderPlayer, count: Math.min(12, 3 + Math.floor(event.impact)),
        color: [0.7, 0.62, 0.5], speed: 0.8 + event.impact * 0.15, spread: 1.2,
        gravity: 8, drag: 2.5, life: [0.2, 0.45], size: 0.13, upBias: 0.4 });
      break;
    case "jumped":
      this.particles.emitBurst({ ...groundUnderPlayer, count: 4, color: [0.7, 0.62, 0.5],
        speed: 1.0, spread: 1.0, gravity: 8, drag: 2.5, life: [0.15, 0.35], size: 0.11 });
      break;
  }
}
```

Note: `ateFood`/`jumped`/`landed` are not positioned in the event union. Two clean options:
1. **Preferred:** store the player's emission point each `sync` (`this.lastPlayerPos`/foot position), and have `handleEvent` read it (the drain loop runs in the same frame, right before `renderer.sync`). This keeps those events un-changed (no balance/test churn) and avoids adding fields to high-traffic motion events. Add a small private helper `crumbPoint()` (eye-ish, in front of camera) and `footPoint()` (player.position, ground). This is the recommendation — only `blockBroken/blockPlaced/mobDied` get coordinate fields.

Add `hexToRgb(hex:number):[number,number,number]` as a tiny local helper (or in `particleSystem.ts`).

## SLICE 2 — Night sky (stars, moon, sun disc, clouds)

**New file: `lib/game/render/starField.ts`** — pure helper + factory split.

Pure helper (unit-testable):
```ts
/** Seeded star directions on a unit sphere (upper hemisphere weighted). */
export function generateStarPositions(count: number, seed: number): Float32Array; // length count*3
```
Uses the same `sin(...)*43758.5453` hash as `chromeTiles.ts` for determinism; no DOM, no THREE. Unit test: deterministic for a seed, length `count*3`, all points on/near the unit sphere (`hypot≈1`).

**New file: `lib/game/render/skyView.ts`** — the THREE factory.

```ts
export type SkyView = {
  sync(state: GameState, timeMs: number): void;
  dispose(): void;
};
export function createSkyView(scene: THREE.Scene, camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera): SkyView;
```

Owns four camera-following objects, all added to `scene` and disposed in `dispose()`:

1. **Stars** — `THREE.Points` from `generateStarPositions(~800, world.seed)` scaled to radius ~900 (inside camera far=2000). `PointsMaterial { size, transparent, depthWrite:false, sizeAttenuation:false }`. Each frame: `points.position.copy(camera.position)` (follow), `material.opacity = clamp01(1 - daylight*2.5)` so they fade in at dusk and vanish by full day. `frustumCulled=false`, `raycast=()=>{}`.
2. **Moon disc** — a small `THREE.Mesh` (circle/sprite quad, `MeshBasicMaterial`/`SpriteMaterial`, soft procedural canvas alpha, off-white). Positioned at the **opposite** of the sun: reuse `sunAngleAt(state.dayClock)`, place at `camera.position + dir(angle + π) * 800`. Always faces camera (Sprite, or `lookAt(camera)`).
3. **Sun disc (optional, recommended)** — same approach, warm color, at `dir(sunAngle)`. The existing `DirectionalLight` has no visible disc; this gives the sun a body. Fade its opacity by `daylight` (hidden at night). Keeps lighting math identical — only adds a visual disc.
4. **Clouds** — a large horizontal `PlaneGeometry` (~600×600) high above (`camera.y + ~120`), `MeshBasicMaterial { map: cloudNoiseTexture(), transparent, opacity ~0.35, depthWrite:false }`. The texture is a runtime canvas noise (`THREE.CanvasTexture`, `wrapS/wrapT = RepeatWrapping`), generated exactly like `chromeTiles.ts noiseTileUrl` (procedural, zero assets). Drift via `material.map.offset.x/y += driftSpeed * dt` over time. Follow `camera.x/z` (not y) so the world border never shows past it; `renderOrder` low, `raycast=()=>{}`.

**Integration in `GameRenderer`:**
- Construct `this.sky = createSkyView(this.scene, this.camera)` in ctor; dispose in `dispose()`.
- Call `this.sky.sync(state, timeMs)` from `sync()`, right after `syncCamera` (so it follows the camera that frame) and near `syncDayNight`. Cloud drift needs the same `dtMs` derived in 1c — pass `timeMs` and let `skyView` keep its own `lastMs`, or pass the renderer's `dtMs`.
- Camera `far` is 2000, fog is 30–200. The procedural sky objects sit at radius ~800–900, beyond fog far, so fog won't fully hide them — but to read clearly, either set `material.fog = false` on sky materials (stars/discs/clouds) so they ignore `scene.fog`, or keep them inside a comfortable range. Set `fog=false` on all sky materials (cleanest).

**Slice 2 testing:**
- Unit: `starField.test.ts` (`generateStarPositions` determinism/shape).
- e2e: `renderedTriangles > 0` unaffected (cloud plane + discs add triangles; stars add points). The console-error fixture proves no THREE warnings. Optionally add a tiny e2e that sets `dayClock` to night via the existing `calmDaytime`-style evaluate and asserts triangles still > 0.
- Manual: scrub to night → stars fade in, moon visible opposite sun, clouds drift.
- Run the full verification command set again.

## SLICE 3 — Weather (engine + renderer + audio)

### 3a. Engine: pure weather system

**Edit `state.ts`** — add the transient field to `GameState` (after `daylight`/`daylightPercent`, near `dayClock`):
```ts
export type WeatherKind = "clear" | "rain" | "snow";
export type WeatherState = { kind: WeatherKind; intensity: number }; // intensity 0..1
// in GameState:
weather: WeatherState;
```
Initialize in `GameEngine.ts:88-119` inline state: `weather: { kind: "clear", intensity: 0 }`.
**Do NOT add `weather` to `serialize()` (`:311`) or `GameSnapshot` (`state.ts:153`)** — it stays transient/session-only, exactly like `mobs`/`cameraMode`. The save round-trip e2e (`smoke.e2e.ts:160`) is unaffected.

**New file: `lib/game/engine/systems/weather.ts`** — pure, DOM-free, deterministic.

```ts
import { WEATHER_CYCLE_SECONDS, WEATHER_RAIN_FRACTION } from "@/lib/game/config";
import { BiomeId } from "@/lib/world";
import type { GameState, WeatherKind } from "../state";

/** Deterministic weather phase from elapsed game time + world seed.
 *  Pure function of dayClock & seed — no internal accumulator — so the engine's
 *  substep catch-up loop (multiple step() calls per frame) yields identical
 *  results regardless of how dt is sliced. */
export function weatherAt(dayClock: number, seed: number): { active: boolean; intensity: number } {
  // Each cycle is a fixed window; a seeded hash of the cycle index decides if
  // it rains, and a smooth ramp gives intensity (fade in/out at the edges).
  const cycle = Math.floor(dayClock / WEATHER_CYCLE_SECONDS);
  const phase = (dayClock % WEATHER_CYCLE_SECONDS) / WEATHER_CYCLE_SECONDS; // 0..1
  const h = hash01(cycle, seed);
  const active = h < WEATHER_RAIN_FRACTION;
  if (!active) return { active: false, intensity: 0 };
  // Triangular envelope: ramps 0→1→0 across the cycle so it eases in and out.
  const intensity = 1 - Math.abs(phase * 2 - 1);
  return { active: true, intensity };
}

function hash01(n: number, seed: number): number {
  const v = Math.sin((n + 1) * 12.9898 + seed * 78.233) * 43758.5453;
  return v - Math.floor(v);
}

/** Snowy biomes get snow, dry biomes stay clear, everything else rains. */
function precipKindFor(biome: BiomeId): WeatherKind {
  if (biome === BiomeId.Mountains) return "snow";
  if (biome === BiomeId.Desert || biome === BiomeId.Ocean) return "clear";
  return "rain";
}

export function tickWeather(state: GameState): void {
  const { active, intensity } = weatherAt(state.dayClock, state.world.seed);
  if (!active) {
    state.weather.kind = "clear";
    state.weather.intensity = 0;
    return;
  }
  const biome = state.world.getBiome(
    Math.floor(state.player.position.x),
    Math.floor(state.player.position.z)
  );
  const kind = precipKindFor(biome);
  state.weather.kind = kind;
  state.weather.intensity = kind === "clear" ? 0 : intensity;
}
```

Determinism note: because `weatherAt` is a pure function of `dayClock` (already advanced by `tickDayNight`) and `seed`, it is **immune to the substep catch-up loop** (`useMinecraftGame.ts:206-211`) — no internal time accumulator to desync. `tickWeather(state)` takes no `dt`.

**Edit `GameEngine.ts:186`** — slot `tickWeather(state)` immediately after `tickDayNight(state, dt)`. Import it at top alongside the other systems.

**Cosmetic-only (explicit):** weather does NOT touch `HOSTILE_SPAWN_BELOW_DAYLIGHT`, `daylight`, mob spawn logic, or any balance threshold. It only writes `state.weather`. This avoids churn in `GameEngine.test.ts` / spawn-director / save tests.

**Edit `config.ts`** — add tunables in the day-night section:
```ts
// Weather (cosmetic, transient — never persisted). Each window is one cycle;
// a seeded hash of the cycle index decides whether it precipitates.
export const WEATHER_CYCLE_SECONDS = 180;
export const WEATHER_RAIN_FRACTION = 0.35; // ~1 in 3 windows precipitate
```

### 3b. Renderer: precipitation view

**New file: `lib/game/render/precipitation.ts`** — a dedicated pooled emitter (reuses `particlePool.ts`, but with continuous spawning + a wrap-around volume, distinct lifecycle from burst particles).

```ts
export type PrecipitationView = {
  sync(state: GameState, dtMs: number, cameraPos: THREE.Vector3): void;
  dispose(): void;
};
export function createPrecipitation(scene: THREE.Scene): PrecipitationView;
```

- Owns its own `createParticlePool()` + a `THREE.Points` (or, for rain streaks, a `LineSegments`/elongated-point look via a tall sprite). Simplest convincing approach: `THREE.Points` with a vertical-streak sprite texture for rain (procedural canvas: a thin bright vertical line) and a soft round flake for snow; swap the material's map/size by `state.weather.kind`.
- Each `sync`: target live count = `intensity * MAX_PRECIP` (e.g. `MAX_PRECIP=300` rain, fewer/slower snow). Spawn the deficit in a box around `cameraPos` (e.g. ±18 x/z, +12..+20 above), with downward velocity (rain fast ~ -22 m/s + slight wind; snow slow ~ -2.5 m/s with horizontal drift). When a particle falls below `cameraPos.y - ~4` or expires, recycle it (re-emit at the top of the volume) so the field follows the camera and never depletes.
- `weather.kind === "clear"` (or `intensity===0`) → ramp count to 0, hide. `paused` → freeze (skip the step).
- `frustumCulled=false`, `raycast=()=>{}`, `depthWrite:false`. Dispose geometry/material/texture.

**Edit `GameRenderer`:**
- Construct `this.precip = createPrecipitation(this.scene)`; dispose in `dispose()`.
- In `sync()`, after `syncCamera`: `if (!state.paused) this.precip.sync(state, dtMs, this.camera.position);`
- **Overcast sky in `syncDayNight`** — read `state.weather`:
  ```ts
  const overcast = state.weather.kind !== "clear" ? state.weather.intensity : 0;
  this.liveSky.copy(this.nightSky).lerp(this.daySky, daylight);
  if (overcast > 0) this.liveSky.lerp(OVERCAST_GRAY, overcast * 0.6); // pull toward gray
  this.scene.fog?.color.copy(this.liveSky);
  if (this.scene.fog instanceof THREE.Fog) {
    this.scene.fog.far = 200 - overcast * 90;  // pull fog in for an enclosed feel
    this.sun.intensity *= 1 - overcast * 0.5;  // dim the sun under cloud
    this.hemiLight.intensity *= 1 - overcast * 0.35;
  }
  ```
  Add `private readonly overcastGray = new THREE.Color(0x6b7480)` constant. (Apply the intensity dampening after the existing `sun.intensity`/`hemiLight.intensity` assignments so it composes cleanly.)

This is read-only on `state.weather`; if a future refactor removes weather, this degrades to clear.

### 3c. Audio: rain loop

**Recommendation: a dedicated looping filtered-noise `BufferSource` controller created in the graph**, with `start/stop/setIntensity`. Justification: scheduled one-shot patter via the existing `backend.play` would (a) burn `MAX_VOICES` (cap 10, `synth.ts:26`) and collide with footsteps/mining, and (b) sound granular. A single steady looping noise source through a lowpass + gain is the lowest-risk continuous bed, fully synthesized (zero assets), isolated from the SFX voice budget, and trivially gated by `setIntensity(0)` when graph is null/clear.

**New file: `lib/game/audio/rainLoop.ts`**:
```ts
export type RainLoop = {
  setIntensity(level: number): void; // 0..1 → gain ramp; 0 ⇒ effectively silent
  dispose(): void;
};
/** A looping white-noise BufferSource → lowpass → gain. Procedural, no assets. */
export function createRainLoop(ctx: AudioContext, destination: AudioNode): RainLoop;
```
- Build a ~2 s noise `AudioBuffer` (fill with `Math.random()*2-1`), `BufferSource{loop:true}` → `BiquadFilter{type:"lowpass", frequency:~1800}` → `GainNode(0)` → `destination` (the `masterGain`, or a new branch off it). `source.start()` once at creation; `setIntensity` ramps `gain.gain` (e.g. `linearRampToValueAtTime`, max ~0.18). `dispose()` stops and disconnects.

**Edit `audioDirector.ts`:**
- In `createDefaultGraph` (`:73-106`): construct `const rain = createRainLoop(ctx, masterGain);` and add `rain` to the returned `AudioGraph`; call `rain.dispose()` in the graph's `dispose()`. Extend `AudioGraph` type (`:45-51`) with `rain: RainLoop`.
- In the director's `sync(state, dt)` (`:234`), after the existing music block: `graph.rain.setIntensity(state.weather.kind === "rain" ? state.weather.intensity : 0);` (snow is silent). Naturally guarded by the existing `if (!graph) return;` at `:235` — graceful when audio is locked/unavailable.
- **Snow = no audio** (intensity → 0).
- **Optional thunder one-shot:** add a `THUNDER_SOUND` to `soundParams.ts` and, in `sync`, when `kind==="rain" && intensity > 0.7`, roll `deps.rng()` against a low per-second probability (scaled by `dt`) to `backend.play(THUNDER_SOUND, { gain: 0.6 })`. Uses the injectable `rng` so it's testable; optional and isolated.

For the **injected fake graph in tests** (`audioDirector.test.ts`), add a no-op `rain: { setIntensity(){}, dispose(){} }` so the type stays satisfied — confirm existing audio tests still pass.

### 3d. Slice 3 testing
- **New unit `lib/game/engine/systems/weather.test.ts`** (`bun:test`, pure):
  - `weatherAt` is deterministic for a given `(dayClock, seed)`; intensity ∈ [0,1]; triangular envelope peaks mid-cycle.
  - Over a swept time range, both clear and active windows occur for a representative seed (rain fraction ≈ `WEATHER_RAIN_FRACTION`).
  - `tickWeather`: snow only in `BiomeId.Mountains`; clear in `Desert`/`Ocean`; rain otherwise (drive with a stub world exposing `getBiome` + `seed`, or a real `VoxelWorld` at a known seed/biome). Substep-invariance: calling `tickWeather` after the same `dayClock` advance gives the same result regardless of dt slicing (it's pure on `dayClock`).

---

## Cross-cutting: complete file manifest

**New files**
- `lib/game/render/particlePool.ts` — pure SoA particle pool: spawn/integrate/retire/recycle.
- `lib/game/render/particlePool.test.ts` — unit tests for the pool.
- `lib/game/render/particleSystem.ts` — THREE.Points factory wrapping the pool; `emitBurst/update/dispose`.
- `lib/game/render/starField.ts` — pure seeded star-position generator + (optionally) the stars helper.
- `lib/game/render/starField.test.ts` — unit test for `generateStarPositions`.
- `lib/game/render/skyView.ts` — `createSkyView` factory: stars, moon, sun disc, drifting clouds.
- `lib/game/render/precipitation.ts` — `createPrecipitation` factory: camera-following rain/snow field.
- `lib/game/engine/systems/weather.ts` — pure deterministic `weatherAt` + `tickWeather`.
- `lib/game/engine/systems/weather.test.ts` — unit tests for weather logic.
- `lib/game/audio/rainLoop.ts` — looping synthesized filtered-noise rain bed.

- `lib/game/engine/state.ts` — add `x,y,z` to `blockBroken/blockPlaced/mobDied`; add `WeatherKind`/`WeatherState`; add `weather` to `GameState` (NOT to `GameSnapshot`).
- `lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts` — include block coords in `blockBroken`(:93)/`blockPlaced`(:124) emits.
- `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts` — include `mob.position` in `mobDied`(:372); init `weather` in initial state; call `tickWeather(state)` after `tickDayNight`(:186).
- `lib/game/config.ts` — `WEATHER_CYCLE_SECONDS`, `WEATHER_RAIN_FRACTION`.
- `lib/game/render/GameRenderer.ts` — own/dispose particle system, sky view, precipitation; add `handleEvent`; derive `dtMs`; footstep dust; overcast in `syncDayNight`.
- `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts` — call `renderer.handleEvent(event)` in the drain loop (~:232).
- `lib/game/audio/audioDirector.ts` — add `rain` to `AudioGraph`, build it in `createDefaultGraph`, drive `setIntensity` from `state.weather` in `sync`; (optional) thunder; dispose rain.
- `lib/game/audio/soundParams.ts` — (optional) `THUNDER_SOUND` ZZFX recipe.
- `lib/game/audio/audioDirector.test.ts` — add no-op `rain` to the injected fake graph if its shape is asserted.

**Docs**
- `AGENTS.md` — "zero binary assets" remains true; note all new visuals are procedural quads/Points/canvas-noise and all new audio is synthesized.
- `docs/architecture.md` — renderer section (particles/sky/precipitation sub-views), audio section (rain loop on the graph), and the new `weather` system in the engine step order; state explicitly that weather is transient (no save/worldgen impact).
- `CHANGELOG.md` — one entry for the juice & atmosphere pass; note NO save-format/worldgen change.

---

## Slice ordering (each independently green)

1. **Slice 1 — Particles.** Pure pool + tests → THREE factory → additive event fields + mining/GameEngine emits → `renderer.handleEvent` + drain wiring → footstep dust. Self-contained; tests stay green (additive fields), e2e renders.
2. **Slice 2 — Night sky.** Pure star helper + test → `skyView` factory → renderer wiring. No engine/save touch; purely visual.
3. **Slice 3 — Weather.** `config.ts` tunables → pure `weather.ts` + test → `state.ts` field + `GameEngine` init/step wiring → renderer precipitation + overcast → audio rain loop. Cosmetic-only, transient, save/worldgen untouched.

Each slice ends with: `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run format:check && bun run test && bun run build`, plus `bun run test:e2e` (smoke) for slices that change rendering.

---

## Risks & mitigations

- **Particle/precip counts & draw calls:** one `THREE.Points` per system (particles, stars, precip) = a few draw calls total. Pool cap 512 (bursts) and ~300 (precip). `depthWrite:false`, `frustumCulled=false`, `raycast=()=>{}` keep them cheap and non-interfering. Recycle-oldest at cap prevents unbounded growth.
- **dt derivation in `renderer.sync`:** `sync` receives absolute `timeMs`. Each render sub-view that needs dt (`particleSystem`, `precipitation`, cloud drift) derives it from a tracked `lastMs`, with the first frame defaulting to ~16 ms and the integrator clamping dt ≤ 0.1 s to absorb tab-stall jumps. Particles freeze while `paused` (skip the step).
- **GPU resource disposal:** every new factory exposes `dispose()` and is freed in `GameRenderer.dispose()` (geometry, material, **and** any `CanvasTexture`). The cloud/star/precip sprite textures are explicitly disposed — mirrors the existing `mobVisuals.dispose` discipline.
- **Weather determinism under substeps:** `weatherAt` is a pure function of `dayClock` + `seed` with no internal accumulator, so the bounded substep catch-up loop (`useMinecraftGame.ts:206-211`) produces identical results regardless of dt slicing. `tickWeather` takes no dt.
- **Audio graceful degradation:** `audio.handleEvent`/`audio.sync` already early-return when `graph` is null. The rain loop lives inside the graph (created only on unlock), and `setIntensity` is only called when `graph` exists — silent and safe before the unlock gesture and in headless tests (injected fake graph).
- **Save/worldgen safety:** `weather` is never added to `serialize()`/`GameSnapshot`; no worldgen call changes. Worldgen SHA-256 hash tests and the save round-trip e2e are untouched. Confirm by re-running `bun test` (includes `generation.test.ts`, `save.test.ts`) and the save round-trip e2e.

---

- /Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts

> AGENT

The plan is written. It's a focused, low-risk **juice & atmosphere** pass in three independently-shippable slices, all pure procedural rendering/audio with **no save-format or worldgen impact**:

1. **Particles** — a pure, unit-tested SoA particle pool + a `THREE.Points` factory; block-break shards (colored from `BLOCK_COLORS`), mob-death puffs, eating crumbs, and jump/land/footstep dust, wired through a new `renderer.handleEvent()` in the existing event drain. Block/mob events get additive `x,y,z` so effects land in the right place.
2. **Night sky** — seeded stars that fade in at dusk, a moon + (newly visible) sun disc, and a drifting procedural-noise cloud layer, all camera-following.
3. **Weather** — a pure, deterministic `weatherAt(dayClock, seed)` system writing a **transient** `state.weather` (never serialized), driving rain/snow precipitation, an overcast sky, and a synthesized rain audio loop. Biome decides rain vs snow; it's strictly cosmetic (no balance thresholds touched).

I grounded every integration point in the real code (the event drain at `useMinecraftGame.ts:213`, the sub-view factory pattern in `GameRenderer`, the emit sites, `world.getBiome`, the audio graph), and the design keeps the worldgen-hash and save round-trip tests untouched as proof of no-impact.

Dispose geometry/\nmaterial/texture.\n\n**Event enrichment (additive)** `state.ts`: add `x,y,z` to `blockBroken`/`blockPlaced`/`mobDied`\n(existing tests assert only `type`+`blockId`/`kind`, so they still pass). Update the three emit\nsites (`mining.ts:93/124`, `GameEngine.ts:372`).\n\n**`GameRenderer`** — own `particles` (ctor + dispose); derive `dtMs` from a tracked `lastSyncMs`;\nadd **`handleEvent(event)`** mapping: `blockBroken`→~10 shards in the block's `BLOCK_COLORS`;\n`blockPlaced`→small puff; `mobDied`→~14 puff in mob color; `ateFood`→crumbs at player; `jumped`/\n`landed`→ground dust scaled by impact. `ateFood/jumped/landed` aren't positioned — read the\nplayer foot/eye point tracked each `sync` (no event change for high-traffic motion events).\n**Footstep dust** spawned in `sync` from a horizontal-travel cadence when on ground (model:\n`audio/footsteps.ts`). Skip the pool step while `paused`.\n\n**`useMinecraftGame.ts`** — add `renderer.handleEvent(event)` in the drain loop beside\n`audio.handleEvent(event)`.\n\n---\n\n## Slice 2 — Night sky (stars · moon · sun disc · clouds)\n\n**`lib/game/render/starField.ts`** — pure `generateStarPositions(count, seed): Float32Array`\n(seeded hash like `chromeTiles.ts`; points on a sphere). **`starField.test.ts`** — determinism/shape.\n\n**`lib/game/render/skyView.ts`** — `createSkyView(scene, camera)` → `{sync(state, timeMs), dispose}`,\nall objects camera-following, `material.fog=false`, `raycast=()=>{}`, freed in dispose:\n- **Stars**: `THREE.Points` (~800) at radius ~900, opacity `clamp01(1 - daylight*2.5)` (fade in at dusk).\n- **Moon + sun disc**: small camera-facing sprites at `sunAngleAt(dayClock)` (sun) and the opposite\n  angle (moon); sun fades by `daylight`, moon by `1-daylight`. (Sun currently has no visible body.)\n- **Clouds**: large semi-transparent plane high above, `CanvasTexture` noise (Repeat wrap), drifting\n  via `map.offset` over dt; follows camera x/z only.\n\n**`GameRenderer`** — own `sky`; call `sky.sync(state, timeMs)` in `sync()` after `syncCamera`.\n\n---\n\n## Slice 3 — Weather (engine · renderer · audio) — cosmetic, transient\n\n**`config.ts`** — `WEATHER_CYCLE_SECONDS = 180`, `WEATHER_RAIN_FRACTION = 0.35`.\n\n**`lib/game/engine/systems/weather.ts`** (pure, DOM-free) —\n`weatherAt(dayClock, seed): {active, intensity}` (seeded hash of the cycle index decides\nprecipitation; triangular envelope ramps intensity 0→1→0 — **pure on `dayClock`+`seed`, so the\nsubstep catch-up loop can't desync it**). `tickWeather(state)` (no dt) sets `state.weather` and\npicks snow (Mountains) / clear (Desert, Ocean) / rain (else) via `world.getBiome`.\n**`weather.test.ts`** — determinism, intensity∈[0,1], biome→kind, rain-fraction sanity.\n\n**`state.ts` / `GameEngine.ts`** — add `WeatherKind`/`WeatherState` and `weather: WeatherState` to\n`GameState`; init `{kind:\"clear\", intensity:0}`; call `tickWeather(state)` after `tickDayNight`.\n**Do NOT add `weather` to `serialize()` or `GameSnapshot`.** **Do NOT touch any spawn/daylight\nthreshold** — weather is cosmetic only (avoids balance/test churn).\n\n**`lib/game/render/precipitation.ts`** — `createPrecipitation(scene)` → `{sync(state, dtMs,\ncameraPos), dispose}`. Own pool + `THREE.Points` (rain = thin vertical streak sprite, fast\ndownward; snow = soft flake, slow drift). Target live count = `intensity * MAX` around the camera;\nrecycle particles that fall below/expire so the field follows the player. `clear`→ramp to 0.\n**`GameRenderer.syncDayNight`** reads `state.weather`: lerp `liveSky` toward an overcast gray, pull\n`fog.far` in, and dampen `sun`/`hemiLight` intensity by intensity.\n\n**`lib/game/audio/rainLoop.ts`** — `createRainLoop(ctx, destination)` → `{setIntensity, dispose}`:\na looping white-noise `BufferSource` → lowpass → gain (synthesized, zero assets, off the SFX voice\nbudget). Add `rain` to `AudioGraph`, build it in `createDefaultGraph`, dispose it there; in\n`audioDirector.sync` call `graph.rain.setIntensity(kind===\"rain\" ? intensity : 0)` (snow silent).\nAdd a no-op `rain` to the injected fake graph in `audioDirector.test.ts`. Optional rare thunder\none-shot (via injectable `rng`) at high intensity.\n\n---\n\n## Files\n\n**New**: `lib/game/render/particlePool.ts` (+test), `particleSystem.ts`, `starField.ts` (+test),\n`skyView.ts`, `precipitation.ts`; `lib/game/engine/systems/weather.ts` (+test);\n`lib/game/audio/rainLoop.ts`.\n**Edit**: `lib/game/engine/state.ts` (event coords, weather type+field), `systems/mining.ts`,\n`engine/GameEngine.ts` (mobDied coords, weather init+step), `config.ts`,\n`render/GameRenderer.ts` (own/dispose new views, `handleEvent`, dtMs, footstep dust, overcast),\n`useMinecraftGame.ts` (drain wiring), `audio/audioDirector.ts` (rain loop), `audio/soundParams.ts`\n(optional thunder), `audio/audioDirector.test.ts` (fake-graph `rain`).\n\n## Verification\n\nPer slice and final: `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`,\n`bun run build`; `bun run test:e2e` (smoke) for the rendering slices.\n- New unit tests: particle pool, star positions, weather system (all pure/deterministic).\n- Confirm **`generation.test.ts`** (worldgen hashes) and **`save.test.ts`** + the save round-trip\n  e2e (`smoke.e2e.ts:160`, `version===3`) still pass — proof of no save/worldgen impact.\n- e2e: `renderedTriangles > 0` and the console-error fixture stay green (breaking a block now also\n  emits a burst; night/weather add objects). Optionally assert triangles>0 with forced night/rain.\n- Manual: break a block → shards; jump/land → dust; scrub to night → stars/moon/clouds; force rain\n  → precipitation + overcast sky + rain bed; confirm pointer-lock/gameplay unaffected and FPS holds.\n\n## Docs (same change)\n\n`CHANGELOG.md` (one entry; note NO save/worldgen impact); `docs/architecture.md` (renderer\nsub-views, audio rain loop, new `weather` system in the step order, weather is transient);\n`AGENTS.md` \"zero binary assets\" stays true (all new visuals/audio procedural).\n\n## Slice order & risks\n\nOrder: **1 Particles → 2 Night sky → 3 Weather** (each independently green). Risks & mitigations:\ndraw calls (one `THREE.Points` per system; caps 512/300; `depthWrite:false`, `raycast` no-op);\ndt derived from tracked `lastSyncMs`, clamped ≤0.1s, frozen while paused; every new GPU resource\n(incl. CanvasTextures) freed in `dispose()`; weather determinism guaranteed by `weatherAt` being\npure on `dayClock`/`seed`; audio degrades gracefully (rain loop lives in the graph, silent before\nunlock / in headless tests).\n",

96	/** Places the selected block against the targeted face, refusing self-entombment. */
97	export function placeSelectedBlock(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {
98	  const { world } = state;
99	  const origin = eyePosition(state, scratchEye);
100	  const direction = lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchDir);
101	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, origin, direction, MINE_REACH);
102	  if (!result) return;
103	
104	  const tx = result.previous.x;
105	  const ty = result.previous.y;
106	  const tz = result.previous.z;
107	  if (!world.inBounds(tx, ty, tz) || world.get(tx, ty, tz) !== BlockId.Air) return;
108	
109	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
110	  if (!slot || !slot.id || slot.kind !== "block" || slot.count <= 0 || slot.blockId === undefined) return;
111	  if (slot.blockId === BlockId.Bedrock) return;
112	
113	  const afterTake = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot);
114	  if (!afterTake) return;
115	  state.inventory = afterTake;
116	  state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty, tz, slot.blockId);
117	  if (collidesAt(world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT)) {
118	    state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty, tz, BlockId.Air);
119	    state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, 1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
120	    return;
121	  }
122	
123	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
124	  emit({ type: "blockPlaced", blockId: slot.blockId });
125	}
126

132	export function createCrackOverlay(scene: THREE.Scene): CrackOverlayView {
133	  const textures = createCrackTextures();
134	  const crackMaterials = textures.map(
135	    (texture) =>
136	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
137	        map: texture,
138	        transparent: true,
139	        depthWrite: false,
140	        polygonOffset: true,
141	        polygonOffsetFactor: -1,
142	        polygonOffsetUnits: -1
143	      })
144	  );
145	  const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.015, 1.015, 1.015);
146	  const overlay = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, crackMaterials[0]);
147	  overlay.visible = false;
148	  overlay.renderOrder = 5;
149	  scene.add(overlay);
150	
151	  return {
152	    update(mining, world) {
153	      if (!mining.targetKey || mining.progress <= 0) {
154	        overlay.visible = false;
155	        return;
156	      }
157	
158	      const [sx, sy, sz] = mining.targetKey.split(",");
159	      const bx = Number.parseInt(sx, 10);
160	      const by = Number.parseInt(sy, 10);
161	      const bz = Number.parseInt(sz, 10);
162	      if (!Number.isFinite(bx) || !Number.isFinite(by) || !Number.isFinite(bz)) {
163	        overlay.visible = false;
164	        return;
165	      }
166	
167	      const block = world.get(bx, by, bz);
168	      if (block === BlockId.Air || block === BlockId.Bedrock) {
169	        overlay.visible = false;
170	        return;
171	      }
172	
173	      const hardness = BREAK_HARDNESS[block as BlockId] ?? 2;
174	      const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.999, mining.progress / hardness));
175	      const stage = Math.min(crackMaterials.length - 1, Math.floor(progress * crackMaterials.length));
176	      overlay.material = crackMaterials[stage];
177	      overlay.position.set(bx + 0.5, by + 0.5, bz + 0.5);
178	      overlay.visible = true;
179	    },
180	
181	    dispose() {
182	      scene.remove(overlay);
183	      geometry.dispose();
184	      for (const material of crackMaterials) material.dispose();
185	      for (const texture of textures) texture.dispose();
186	    }
187	  };
188	}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/render/particlePool.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * A pure, DOM-free particle simulation: a fixed structure-of-arrays pool that\n * the THREE wiring (particleSystem.ts) uploads to GPU buffers each frame.\n * Kept free of Three.js and of randomness (callers supply jitter) so it runs\n * and is unit-tested under `bun test`.\n */\n\nexport const PARTICLE_CAP = 512;\n\nexport type ParticlePool = {\n  readonly cap: number;\n  /** Live particles occupy slots [0, count); update() keeps them front-packed. */\n  count: number;\n  px: Float32Array;\n  py: Float32Array;\n  pz: Float32Array;\n  vx: Float32Array;\n  vy: Float32Array;\n  vz: Float32Array;\n  age: Float32Array;\n  life: Float32Array;\n  size: Float32Array;\n  cr: Float32Array;\n  cg: Float32Array;\n  cb: Float32Array;\n  gravity: Float32Array;\n  drag: Float32Array;\n};\n\nexport type EmitParticle = {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  z: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  vz: number;\n  life: number;\n  size: number;\n  r: number;\n  g: number;\n  b: number;\n  gravity?: number;\n  drag?: number;\n};\n\nexport function createParticlePool(cap: number = PARTICLE_CAP): ParticlePool {\n  return {\n    cap,\n    count: 0,\n    px: new Float32Array(cap),\n    py: new Float32Array(cap),\n    pz: new Float32Array(cap),\n    vx: new Float32Array(cap),\n    vy: new Float32Array(cap),\n    vz: new Float32Array(cap),\n    age: new Float32Array(cap),\n    life: new Float32Array(cap),\n    size: new Float32Array(cap),\n    cr: new Float32Array(cap),\n    cg: new Float32Array(cap),\n    cb: new Float32Array(cap),\n    gravity: new Float32Array(cap),\n    drag: new Float32Array(cap)\n  };\n}\n\nfunction writeSlot(pool: ParticlePool, i: number, p: EmitParticle): void {\n  pool.px[i] = p.x;\n  pool.py[i] = p.y;\n  pool.pz[i] = p.z;\n  pool.vx[i] = p.vx;\n  pool.vy[i] = p.vy;\n  pool.vz[i] = p.vz;\n  pool.age[i] = 0;\n  pool.life[i] = p.life;\n  pool.size[i] = p.size;\n  pool.cr[i] = p.r;\n  pool.cg[i] = p.g;\n  pool.cb[i] = p.b;\n  pool.gravity[i] = p.gravity ?? 0;\n  pool.drag[i] = p.drag ?? 0;\n}\n\nfunction copySlot(pool: ParticlePool, from: number, to: number): void {\n  pool.px[to] = pool.px[from];\n  pool.py[to] = pool.py[from];\n  pool.pz[to] = pool.pz[from];\n  pool.vx[to] = pool.vx[from];\n  pool.vy[to] = pool.vy[from];\n  pool.vz[to] = pool.vz[from];\n  pool.age[to] = pool.age[from];\n  pool.life[to] = pool.life[from];\n  pool.size[to] = pool.size[from];\n  pool.cr[to] = pool.cr[from];\n  pool.cg[to] = pool.cg[from];\n  pool.cb[to] = pool.cb[from];\n  pool.gravity[to] = pool.gravity[from];\n  pool.drag[to] = pool.drag[from];\n}\n\n/** Adds one particle. At capacity it recycles slot 0 (drop-oldest) — cosmetic\n *  bursts don't care about exact ordering, and this never overflows. */\nexport function spawnParticle(pool: ParticlePool, p: EmitParticle): void {\n  if (pool.count < pool.cap) {\n    writeSlot(pool, pool.count, p);\n    pool.count += 1;\n  } else {\n    writeSlot(pool, 0, p);\n  }\n}\n\n/** Integrates `dt` seconds: ages particles, applies gravity + drag, and retires\n *  expired ones by swap-removal so live particles stay packed in [0, count).\n *  `dt` is clamped so a stalled/backgrounded tab can't teleport everything. */\nexport function updateParticlePool(pool: ParticlePool, dt: number): void {\n  const d = Math.max(0, Math.min(dt, 0.1));\n  let i = 0;\n  while (i < pool.count) {\n    pool.age[i] += d;\n    if (pool.age[i] >= pool.life[i]) {\n      const last = pool.count - 1;\n      if (i !== last) copySlot(pool, last, i);\n      pool.count = last;\n      continue; // re-process the slot that was swapped in\n    }\n    const dragF = Math.max(0, 1 - pool.drag[i] * d);\n    pool.vy[i] -= pool.gravity[i] * d;\n    pool.vx[i] *= dragF;\n    pool.vy[i] *= dragF;\n    pool.vz[i] *= dragF;\n    pool.px[i] += pool.vx[i] * d;\n    pool.py[i] += pool.vy[i] * d;\n    pool.pz[i] += pool.vz[i] * d;\n    i += 1;\n  }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/render/particlePool.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, test } from \"bun:test\";\nimport { createParticlePool, type EmitParticle, PARTICLE_CAP, spawnParticle, updateParticlePool } from \"@/lib/game/render/particlePool\";\n\nfunction sample(overrides: Partial<EmitParticle> = {}): EmitParticle {\n  return { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, vx: 1, vy: 2, vz: 0, life: 1, size: 0.1, r: 0.5, g: 0.5, b: 0.5, ...overrides };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"particlePool\", () => {\n  test(\"starts empty with cap-sized buffers\", () => {\n    const pool = createParticlePool();\n    expect(pool.cap).toBe(PARTICLE_CAP);\n    expect(pool.count).toBe(0);\n    expect(pool.px.length).toBe(PARTICLE_CAP);\n    expect(pool.life.length).toBe(PARTICLE_CAP);\n  });\n\n  test(\"spawn fills sequential slots and integrates with gravity + drag\", () => {\n    const pool = createParticlePool(8);\n    spawnParticle(pool, sample({ vx: 1, vy: 0, gravity: 10, drag: 0 }));\n    expect(pool.count).toBe(1);\n\n    updateParticlePool(pool, 0.1);\n    expect(pool.px[0]).toBeCloseTo(0.1, 5); // moved by vx*dt\n    expect(pool.vy[0]).toBeCloseTo(-1, 5); // gravity pulled vy down\n    expect(pool.age[0]).toBeCloseTo(0.1, 5);\n  });\n\n  test(\"retires expired particles via swap-removal and keeps the pool packed\", () => {\n    const pool = createParticlePool(8);\n    spawnParticle(pool, sample({ life: 0.05 })); // will expire\n    spawnParticle(pool, sample({ life: 10, vx: 0, vy: 0, x: 7 })); // survives\n    expect(pool.count).toBe(2);\n\n    updateParticlePool(pool, 0.1);\n    expect(pool.count).toBe(1);\n    // The survivor was swapped into slot 0.\n    expect(pool.px[0]).toBeCloseTo(7, 5);\n  });\n\n  test(\"spawning past cap recycles instead of overflowing\", () => {\n    const pool = createParticlePool(3);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i += 1) spawnParticle(pool, sample({ x: i }));\n    expect(pool.count).toBe(3);\n    // Last spawn overwrote slot 0 (drop-oldest).\n    expect(pool.px[0]).toBe(9);\n  });\n\n  test(\"clamps a huge dt so a stalled tab can't teleport particles\", () => {\n    const pool = createParticlePool(4);\n    spawnParticle(pool, sample({ vx: 1, vy: 0, gravity: 0, drag: 0, life: 100 }));\n    updateParticlePool(pool, 999);\n    // dt clamped to 0.1 → moved only 0.1, not 999.\n    expect(pool.px[0]).toBeCloseTo(0.1, 5);\n  });\n\n  test(\"is deterministic — no internal randomness\", () => {\n    const a = createParticlePool(4);\n    const b = createParticlePool(4);\n    const p = sample({ vx: 0.7, vy: 1.3, gravity: 9, drag: 1.2, life: 5 });\n    spawnParticle(a, p);\n    spawnParticle(b, p);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i += 1) {\n      updateParticlePool(a, 0.03);\n      updateParticlePool(b, 0.03);\n    }\n    expect(a.px[0]).toBe(b.px[0]);\n    expect(a.vy[0]).toBe(b.vy[0]);\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/render/particleSystem.ts",
  "content": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport { createParticlePool, PARTICLE_CAP, spawnParticle, updateParticlePool } from \"./particlePool\";\n\n/**\n * GPU wrapper around the pure particlePool: one THREE.Points draw call whose\n * position/color/size/alpha attributes are refilled from the pool each frame.\n * A small ShaderMaterial draws each particle as a soft round sprite that fades\n * out over its life — no texture, so it stays a zero-asset effect. emitBurst()\n * is the only place randomness enters, keeping the pool itself deterministic.\n */\n\nexport type BurstOptions = {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  z: number;\n  count: number;\n  color: [number, number, number];\n  speed: number;\n  spread?: number;\n  life?: [number, number];\n  size?: number;\n  gravity?: number;\n  drag?: number;\n  upBias?: number;\n  colorJitter?: number;\n};\n\nexport type ParticleSystem = {\n  emitBurst(opts: BurstOptions): void;\n  update(dtMs: number): void;\n  dispose(): void;\n};\n\nexport function hexToRgb(hex: number): [number, number, number] {\n  return [((hex >> 16) & 0xff) / 255, ((hex >> 8) & 0xff) / 255, (hex & 0xff) / 255];\n}\n\nconst VERTEX_SHADER = /* glsl */ `\n  attribute vec3 acolor;\n  attribute float psize;\n  attribute float palpha;\n  varying vec3 vColor;\n  varying float vAlpha;\n  uniform float uScale;\n  void main() {\n    vColor = acolor;\n    vAlpha = palpha;\n    vec4 mv = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);\n    gl_PointSize = max(1.0, psize * uScale / -mv.z);\n    gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv;\n  }\n`;\n\nconst FRAGMENT_SHADER = /* glsl */ `\n  precision mediump float;\n  varying vec3 vColor;\n  varying float vAlpha;\n  void main() {\n    vec2 d = gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5);\n    float r = dot(d, d);\n    if (r > 0.25) discard;\n    float edge = smoothstep(0.25, 0.04, r);\n    gl_FragColor = vec4(vColor, vAlpha * edge);\n  }\n`;\n\nexport function createParticleSystem(scene: THREE.Scene): ParticleSystem {\n  const pool = createParticlePool();\n  const cap = pool.cap;\n\n  const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n  const position = new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(cap * 3), 3);\n  const acolor = new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(cap * 3), 3);\n  const psize = new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(cap), 1);\n  const palpha = new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(cap), 1);\n  position.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);\n  acolor.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);\n  psize.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);\n  palpha.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);\n  geometry.setAttribute(\"position\", position);\n  geometry.setAttribute(\"acolor\", acolor);\n  geometry.setAttribute(\"psize\", psize);\n  geometry.setAttribute(\"palpha\", palpha);\n  geometry.setDrawRange(0, 0);\n\n  const material = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({\n    uniforms: { uScale: { value: 620 } },\n    vertexShader: VERTEX_SHADER,\n    fragmentShader: FRAGMENT_SHADER,\n    transparent: true,\n    depthWrite: false\n  });\n\n  const points = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);\n  points.frustumCulled = false;\n  points.raycast = () => {};\n  points.renderOrder = 4;\n  scene.add(points);\n\n  return {\n    emitBurst(opts) {\n      const spread = opts.spread ?? 1;\n      const [lifeMin, lifeMax] = opts.life ?? [0.3, 0.6];\n      const size = opts.size ?? 0.14;\n      const jitter = opts.colorJitter ?? 0;\n      const upBias = opts.upBias ?? 0;\n      for (let n = 0; n < opts.count; n += 1) {\n        // Random direction on a sphere, scaled by speed + per-particle spread.\n        const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n        const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);\n        const sp = opts.speed * (0.5 + Math.random());\n        spawnParticle(pool, {\n          x: opts.x,\n          y: opts.y,\n          z: opts.z,\n          vx: Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta) * sp + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread,\n          vy: Math.cos(phi) * sp + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread + upBias,\n          vz: Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta) * sp + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread,\n          life: lifeMin + Math.random() * (lifeMax - lifeMin),\n          size: size * (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.6),\n          r: clamp01(opts.color[0] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * jitter),\n          g: clamp01(opts.color[1] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * jitter),\n          b: clamp01(opts.color[2] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * jitter),\n          gravity: opts.gravity ?? 0,\n          drag: opts.drag ?? 0\n        });\n      }\n    },\n\n    update(dtMs) {\n      updateParticlePool(pool, dtMs / 1000);\n      const n = pool.count;\n      const pos = position.array as Float32Array;\n      const col = acolor.array as Float32Array;\n      const sz = psize.array as Float32Array;\n      const al = palpha.array as Float32Array;\n      for (let i = 0; i < n; i += 1) {\n        pos[i * 3] = pool.px[i];\n        pos[i * 3 + 1] = pool.py[i];\n        pos[i * 3 + 2] = pool.pz[i];\n        col[i * 3] = pool.cr[i];\n        col[i * 3 + 1] = pool.cg[i];\n        col[i * 3 + 2] = pool.cb[i];\n        sz[i] = pool.size[i];\n        // Fade out over the back half of life.\n        const t = pool.age[i] / pool.life[i];\n        al[i] = t < 0.5 ? 1 : Math.max(0, 1 - (t - 0.5) * 2);\n      }\n      geometry.setDrawRange(0, n);\n      if (n > 0) {\n        position.needsUpdate = true;\n        acolor.needsUpdate = true;\n        psize.needsUpdate = true;\n        palpha.needsUpdate = true;\n      }\n    },\n\n    dispose() {\n      scene.remove(points);\n      geometry.dispose();\n      material.dispose();\n    }\n  };\n}\n\nfunction clamp01(v: number): number {\n  return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));\n}\n\nexport const PARTICLE_SYSTEM_CAP = PARTICLE_CAP;\n"
}

[0.6, 0.6, 0.6],\n          speed: 3.2,\n          spread: 1.0,\n          gravity: 14,\n          drag: 1.6,\n          life: [0.35, 0.7],\n          size: 0.16,\n          colorJitter: 0.08\n        });\n        break;\n      case \"blockPlaced\":\n        this.particles.emitBurst({\n          x: event.x + 0.5,\n          y: event.y + 0.5,\n          z: event.z + 0.5,\n          count: 6,\n          color: BLOCK_COLORS[event.blockId] ??        break;\n      case \"mobDied\":\n        this.particles.emitBurst({\n          x: event.x,\n          y: event.y + 0.4,\n          z: event.z,\n          count: 14,\n          color: hexToRgb(MOB_TEMPLATES[event.kind].modelArgs[0] as number),\n          speed: 2.6,\n          spread: 1.4,\n          gravity: 10,\n          drag: 1.4,\n          life: [0.4, 0.8],\n          size: state.player.position.z,\n          count: 5,\n          color: [0.82, 0.6, 0.4],\n          speed: 1.6,\n          spread: 0.8,\n          gravity: 12,\n          drag: 1.5,\n          life: [0.25,     z: state.player.position.z,\n          count: Math.min(12, 3 + Math.floor(event.impact)),\n          color: [0.7, 0.62, 0.5],\n          speed: 0.8 + event.impact * 0.12,\n          spread: 1.2,\n this.particles.emitBurst({\n          x: state.player.position.x,\n          y: state.player.position.y,\n          z: state.player.position.z,\n          count: 4,\n          color: [0.7, 0.62, Kicks a small dust puff every couple of strides while walking on the ground. */\n  private emitFootstepDust(state: GameState): void {\n    const { player } = state;\n    if (state.isDead || !player.onGround || Number.isNaN(this.lastFootX)) {\n      this.lastFootX = player.position.x;\n      this.lastFootZ = player.position.z;\n      return;\n    }\n    this.dustDistance += {

A\nsmall ShaderMaterial draws each particle as a soft round sprite that fades\nover its life — no texture, so it stays a zero-asset effect.\n\nThe renderer now exposes handleEvent(event, state), wired into the event\ndrain alongside audio.handleEvent: blockBroken throws shards tinted from\nBLOCK_COLORS, blockPlaced puffs, mobDied bursts in the mob's body color,\nand ateFood/jumped/landed kick dust (landing scales with impact). Footstep\ndust is spawned from a stride-distance accumulator in sync(); particles\nfreeze while paused. The three positioned events (blockBroken, blockPlaced,\nmobDied) gain additive x,y,z fields so bursts land in the right place —\nadditive, so existing event assertions are unaffected.\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1",
  "description": "Commit Slice 1"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/render/starField.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Pure, deterministic star-direction generator (no Three.js, unit-tested).\n * Returns `count` points on the upper unit sphere, which skyView.ts scales out\n * to a large radius and follows the camera.\n */\n\nfunction hash01(n: number, seed: number): number {\n  const v = Math.sin((n + 1) * 12.9898 + seed * 78.233) * 43758.5453;\n  return v - Math.floor(v);\n}\n\nexport function generateStarPositions(count: number, seed: number): Float32Array {\n  const out = new Float32Array(count * 3);\n  for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {\n    const theta = hash01(i * 2 + 1, seed) * Math.PI * 2;\n    // Bias toward overhead (y in 0.08..0.98) so stars don't clutter the horizon.\n    const y = 0.08 + hash01(i * 2 + 2, seed) * 0.9;\n    const r = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - y * y));\n    out[i * 3] = Math.cos(theta) * r;\n    out[i * 3 + 1] = y;\n    out[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(theta) * r;\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n"
}
```
